We further extend the pipeline to action-conditioned world model generation in the spirit of Genie3. Project Page: https://github.com/thu-ml/Causal-Forcing and https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM .

</details>


### [107] [Does Synthetic Layered Design Data Benefit Layered Design Decomposition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15167)
*Kam Man Wu,Haolin Yang,Qingyu Chen,Yihu Tang,Jingye Chen,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了纯合成分层数据是否能够改善图形设计解构，基于CLD基线构建了SynLayers合成数据集，并通过数据驱动实验证明了纯合成数据训练的模型能够超越非可扩展替代方案，性能随数据量增加而提升（约50K样本趋于饱和），且合成数据能避免真实数据集中图层数量分布不均的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成技术虽能产生高质量图像，但输出结果是扁平化的，前景、背景和文本被固定在同一画布中，导致后期灵活编辑困难。现有方法要么依赖稀缺的专有分层资产，要么基于有限结构先验构建部分合成数据，两者在可扩展性上均面临根本性挑战。因此，作者希望探索纯合成分层数据是否能够解决这一"最后一公里"问题。

Method: 基于CLD基线（最先进的图层分解框架）进行数据驱动研究。首先构建了纯合成数据集SynLayers，然后使用视觉语言模型生成文本监督信息，并利用VLM预测的边界框自动化推理输入。通过在不同规模、不同数据来源（合成vs真实）下训练模型，系统性地比较性能差异。

Result: 三项关键发现：(1)纯合成数据训练的模型能够超越非可扩展替代方案（如广泛使用的PrismLayersPro数据集），证明合成数据是可行且有效的替代方案；(2)性能随训练数据规模增加而持续提升，但在约50K样本时增益开始饱和；(3)合成数据能够对图层数量分布进行平衡控制，避免了真实数据集中常见的图层数量分布不均问题。

Conclusion: 纯合成分层数据可以作为分层设计编辑系统的实用基础，作者希望这项数据驱动研究能够鼓励更广泛地采用合成数据来支撑分层设计编辑系统。

Abstract: Recent advances in image generation have made it easy to produce high-quality images. However, these outputs are inherently flattened, entangling foreground elements, background, and text within a fixed canvas. As a result, flexible post-generation editing remains challenging, revealing a clear last-mile gap toward practical usability. Existing approaches either rely on scarce proprietary layered assets or construct partially synthetic data from limited structural priors. However, both strategies face fundamental challenges in scalability. In this work, we investigate whether pure synthetic layered data can improve graphic design decomposition. We make the assumption that, in graphic design, effective decomposition does not require modeling inter-layer dependencies as precisely as in natural-image composition, since design elements are often intentionally arranged as modular and semantically separable components. Concretely, we conduct a data-centric study based on CLD baseline, which is a state-of-the-art layer decomposition framework. Based on the baseline, we construct our own synthetic dataset, SynLayers, generate textual supervision using vision language models, and automate inference inputs with VLM-predicted bounding boxes. Our study reveals three key findings: (1) even training with purely synthetic data can outperform non-scalable alternatives such as the widely used PrismLayersPro dataset, demonstrating its viability as a scalable and effective substitute; (2) performance consistently improves with increased training data scale, while gains begin to saturate at around 50K samples; and (3) synthetic data enables balanced control over layer-count distributions, avoiding the layer-count imbalance commonly observed in real-world datasets. We hope this data-centric study encourages broader adoption of synthetic data as a practical foundation for layered design editing systems.

</details>


### [108] [Evidential Reasoning Advances Interpretable Real-World Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15171)
*Chenyu Lian,Hong-Yu Zhou,Jing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviScreen是一个基于证据推理的疾病筛查框架，通过从双知识库中检索历史病例的区域级证据，结合当前病例进行推理预测，并在对比检索基础上生成异常图以增强定位可解释性，在真实疾病筛查基准上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数基于医学影像的疾病筛查模型可解释性有限且性能欠佳，缺乏引用历史病例和提供透明推理路径的有效机制。

Method: EviScreen框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 从双知识库中检索历史病例的区域级证据，提供回溯性可解释性；(2) 基于证据感知的推理模块，结合当前病例与历史证据进行预测；(3) 利用对比检索生成异常图而非事后显著图，增强定位可解释性。

Result: 在真实疾病筛查基准上取得优越性能，在临床级召回率下实现了显著更高的特异性。

Conclusion: EviScreen通过引入可检索的历史病例证据和对比异常图机制，有效提升了疾病筛查模型的性能与可解释性，为临床可信任的AI辅助诊断提供了新思路。

Abstract: Disease screening is critical for early detection and timely intervention in clinical practice. However, most current screening models for medical images suffer from limited interpretability and suboptimal performance. They often lack effective mechanisms to reference historical cases or provide transparent reasoning pathways. To address these challenges, we introduce EviScreen, an evidential reasoning framework for disease screening that leverages region-level evidence from historical cases. The proposed EviScreen offers retrospection interpretability through regional evidence retrieved from dual knowledge banks. Using this evidential mechanism, the subsequent evidence-aware reasoning module makes predictions using both the current case and evidence from historical cases, thereby enhancing disease screening performance. Furthermore, rather than relying on post-hoc saliency maps, EviScreen enhances localization interpretability by leveraging abnormality maps derived from contrastive retrieval. Our method achieves superior performance on our carefully established benchmarks for real-world disease screening, yielding notably higher specificity at clinical-level recall. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DopamineLcy/EviScreen.

</details>


### [109] [SANA-WM: Efficient Minute-Scale World Modeling with Hybrid Linear Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15178)
*Haoyi Zhu,Haozhe Liu,Yuyang Zhao,Tian Ye,Junsong Chen,Jincheng Yu,Tong He,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SANA-WM是一个26亿参数的开源世界模型，原生支持一分钟长视频生成，具备720p高保真画质和精确相机控制能力。通过混合线性注意力、双分支相机控制、两阶段生成流水线和鲁棒标注管道四大核心设计，在仅用约21.3万公开视频片段训练15天的情况下，实现了与大规模工业基线相当的视觉质量，同时吞吐量提升36倍。其蒸馏变体可在单张RTX 5090上34秒完成60秒720p视频的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在长视频生成（分钟级）方面存在两大瓶颈：一是计算成本高昂，难以支持长时间序列的高效建模；二是缺乏精确的相机控制能力，无法实现6自由度轨迹的精确跟随。此外，开源模型在视觉质量和推理效率上远落后于闭源工业方案。作者旨在构建一个高效、开源、支持分钟级视频生成且具备精确相机控制的世界模型。

Method: SANA-WM采用四项核心技术：(1)混合线性注意力，结合逐帧门控DeltaNet和softmax注意力实现内存高效的长上下文建模；(2)双分支相机控制，确保精确的6自由度轨迹跟随；(3)两阶段生成流水线，对第一阶段输出应用长视频精炼器以提升序列质量和一致性；(4)鲁棒标注管道，从公开视频中提取精确的公制尺度6自由度相机位姿，生成高质量时空一致的动作标签。

Result: 仅使用约21.3万公开视频片段完成训练，在64块H100上训练15天，单GPU即可生成60秒视频；蒸馏变体在单张RTX 5090上配合NVFP4量化，34秒即可去噪生成60秒720p视频。在分钟级世界模型基准上，动作跟随准确率优于现有开源基线，视觉质量与大规模工业基线相当，吞吐量提升36倍。

Conclusion: SANA-WM证明了通过精心设计的架构和训练策略，可以在显著降低数据和计算资源需求的同时，实现分钟级、高保真、带精确相机控制的世界模型，为可扩展世界建模提供了高效的开源解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce SANA-WM, an efficient 2.6B-parameter open-source world model natively trained for one-minute generation, synthesizing high-fidelity, 720p, minute-scale videos with precise camera control. SANA-WM achieves visual quality comparable to large-scale industrial baselines such as LingBot-World and HY-WorldPlay, while significantly improving efficiency. Four core designs drive our architecture: (1) Hybrid Linear Attention combines frame-wise Gated DeltaNet (GDN) with softmax attention for memory-efficient long-context modeling. (2) Dual-Branch Camera Control ensures precise 6-DoF trajectory adherence. (3) Two-Stage Generation Pipeline applies a long-video refiner to stage-1 outputs, improving quality and consistency across sequences. (4) Robust Annotation Pipeline extracts accurate metric-scale 6-DoF camera poses from public videos to yield high-quality, spatiotemporally consistent action labels. Driven by these designs, SANA-WMdemonstrates remarkable efficiency across data, training compute, and inference hardware: it uses only $\sim$213K public video clips with metric-scale pose supervision, completes training in 15 days on 64 H100s, and generates each 60s clip on a single GPU; its distilled variant can be deployed on a single RTX 5090 with NVFP4 quantization to denoise a 60s 720p clip in 34s. On our one-minute world-model benchmark, SANA-WM demonstrates stronger action-following accuracy than prior open-source baselines and achieves comparable visual quality at $36\times$ higher throughput for scalable world modeling.

</details>


### [110] [From Plans to Pixels: Learning to Plan and Orchestrate for Open-Ended Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15181)
*Anirudh Sundara Rajan,Krishna Kumar Singh,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于经验学习的长期图像编辑框架，通过规划器生成原子分解、编排器选择工具和区域执行步骤，并结合视觉语言评判器的奖励信号来训练编排器、优化规划器，实现比单步或规则多步方法更连贯可靠的编辑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型处理抽象、多步骤指令（如"让这个广告更素食友好"）时效果不佳；基于智能体的方法依赖手工流程或教师模仿，缺乏灵活性，且学习与实际编辑效果脱钩。

Method: 提出经验式框架：1) 规划器生成结构化的原子步骤分解；2) 编排器为每一步选择工具和编辑区域；3) 视觉语言评判器提供基于结果（编辑效果）的奖励信号（指令遵循度+视觉质量）；4) 编排器通过最大化奖励进行训练；5) 成功的轨迹用于优化规划器。

Result: 该方法相比单步或基于规则的多步基线方法，能够生成更连贯、更可靠的编辑结果。

Conclusion: 将规划与基于奖励的执行紧密耦合，是提升长期、抽象图像编辑任务性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Modern image editing models produce realistic results but struggle with abstract, multi step instructions (e.g., ``make this advertisement more vegetarian-friendly''). Prior agent based methods decompose such tasks but rely on handcrafted pipelines or teacher imitation, limiting flexibility and decoupling learning from actual editing outcomes. We propose an experiential framework for long-horizon image editing, where a planner generates structured atomic decompositions and an orchestrator selects tools and regions to execute each step. A vision language judge provides outcome-based rewards for instruction adherence and visual quality. The orchestrator is trained to maximize these rewards, and successful trajectories are used to refine the planner. By tightly coupling planning with reward driven execution, our approach yields more coherent and reliable edits than single-step or rule-based multistep baselines.

</details>


### [111] [Warp-as-History: Generalizable Camera-Controlled Video Generation from One Training Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15182)
*Yifan Wang,Tong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Warp-as-History方法，通过将相机运动引起的图像扭曲作为伪历史输入到冻结的视频生成模型中，无需训练或架构修改即可实现零样本的相机可控视频生成，结合轻量级LoRA微调可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机可控视频生成方法通常需要在大规模相机标注视频上进行后训练，或需要在测试时进行优化/额外去噪指导，这些方法存在计算成本高、泛化性受限等问题。

Method: 提出Warp-as-History接口：1）根据目标相机轨迹，将过去帧通过相机扭曲生成伪历史帧；2）将伪历史帧的位置编码与正在去噪的目标帧对齐；3）移除没有有效源观测的扭曲历史令牌。该方法无需训练、架构修改或测试时优化，也可结合轻量级LoRA微调进一步提升性能。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验证明了该方法的有效性，零样本即可实现相机轨迹跟随能力，LoRA微调后进一步提升了相机一致性、视觉质量和运动动态。

Conclusion: Warp-as-History作为一种简单接口，揭示了冻结视频生成模型无需训练即可具备零样本相机轨迹跟随能力，为相机可控视频生成提供了高效、无需后训练/测试时优化的新范式。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation has made substantial progress, enabling generated videos to follow prescribed viewpoint trajectories. However, existing methods usually learn camera-specific conditioning through camera encoders, control branches, or attention and positional-encoding modifications, which often require post-training on large-scale camera-annotated videos. Training-free alternatives avoid such post-training, but often shift the cost to test-time optimization or extra denoising-time guidance. We propose Warp-as-History, a simple interface that turns camera-induced warps into camera-warped pseudo-history with target-frame positional alignment and visible-token selection. Given a target camera trajectory, we construct camera-warped pseudo-history from past observations and feed it through the model's visual-history pathway. Crucially, we align its positional encoding with the target frames being denoised and remove warped-history tokens without valid source observations. Without any training, architectural modification, or test-time optimization, this interface reveals a non-trivial zero-shot capability of a frozen video generation model to follow camera trajectories. Moreover, lightweight offline LoRA finetuning on only one camera-annotated video further improves this capability and generalizes to unseen videos, improving camera adherence, visual quality, and motion dynamics without test-time optimization or target-video adaptation. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets confirm the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [112] [Quantitative Video World Model Evaluation for Geometric-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15185)
*Jiaxin Wu,Yihao Pi,Yinling Zhang,Yuheng Li,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PDI-Bench，一个用于量化评估生成视频中几何一致性的框架，通过分割、点跟踪和3D重建计算投影几何残差，从尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性和3D结构刚性三个维度检测几何故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频评估方法过度依赖人工判断或可学习的评分器，主观性强且对几何失效的诊断能力弱，缺乏量化评估生成视频物理合理性的工具。

Method: 通过分割和点跟踪（如SAM 2、MegaSaM、CoTracker3）获取物体级别观测，利用单目重建将其提升到3D世界空间，计算一组投影几何残差，涵盖尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性和3D结构刚性三个失效维度。同时构建PDI-Dataset支持系统评估。

Result: 在多个先进视频生成器上，PDI揭示了传统感知指标无法捕获的、与几何相关的系统性故障模式，提供了诊断信号。

Conclusion: PDI-Bench为生成视频的几何合理性提供了量化诊断工具，有助于推动物理合理的视频生成和物理世界模型的发展。

Abstract: Generative video models are increasingly studied as implicit world models, yet evaluating whether they produce physically plausible 3D structure and motion remains challenging. Most existing video evaluation pipelines rely heavily on human judgment or learned graders, which can be subjective and weakly diagnostic for geometric failures. We introduce PDI-Bench (Perspective Distortion Index), a quantitative framework for auditing geometric coherence in generated videos. Given a generated clip, we obtain object-centric observations via segmentation and point tracking (e.g., SAM 2, MegaSaM, and CoTracker3), lift them to 3D world-space coordinates via monocular reconstruction, and compute a set of projective-geometry residuals capturing three failure dimensions: scale-depth alignment, 3D motion consistency, and 3D structural rigidity. To support systematic evaluation, we build PDI-Dataset, covering diverse scenarios designed to stress these geometric constraints. Across state-of-the-art video generators, PDI reveals consistent geometry-specific failure modes that are not captured by common perceptual metrics, and provides a diagnostic signal for progress toward physically grounded video generation and physical world model. Our code and dataset can be found at https://pdi-bench.github.io/.

</details>


### [113] [Articraft: An Agentic System for Scalable Articulated 3D Asset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15187)
*Matt Zhou,Ruining Li,Xiaoyang Lyu,Zhaomou Song,Zhening Huang,Chuanxia Zheng,Christian Rupprecht,Andrea Vedaldi,Shangzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Articraft系统，利用大语言模型通过程序生成（编写代码）的方式，大规模创建高质量的可动3D资产，并构建了包含10K+资产的Articraft-10K数据集，在多个下游任务中验证了其效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习理解可动3D物体的瓶颈在于缺乏大规模、多样化的数据集。现有数据集规模有限，难以支撑相关模型训练和下游应用。

Method: 将可动3D资产生成问题转化为编写程序（代码）的问题；设计Articraft智能体系统，包含领域特定SDK（定义部件、组合几何、指定关节、编写测试）和受限工作空间/接口的调度框架，LLM在该框架下编写代码生成资产，系统验证并返回结构化反馈。

Result: Articraft生成的可动3D资产质量优于现有的可动资产生成器和通用编码智能体；构建了Articraft-10K数据集（10K+资产，245个类别），在可动资产模型训练、机器人仿真和虚拟现实等下游任务中展示了实用性。

Conclusion: 利用LLM通过程序合成方式生成可动3D资产是可行且有效的，能够弥补现有数据集的不足，为相关研究和应用提供大规模高质量数据支持。

Abstract: A bottleneck in learning to understand articulated 3D objects is the lack of large and diverse datasets. In this paper, we propose to leverage large language models (LLMs) to close this gap and generate articulated assets at scale. We reduce the problem of generating an articulated 3D asset to that of writing a program that builds it. We then introduce a new agentic system, Articraft, that writes such programs automatically. We design a programmatic interface and harness to help the LLM do so effectively. The LLM writes code against a domain-specific SDK for defining parts, composing geometry, specifying joints, and writing tests to validate the resulting assets. The harness exposes a restricted workspace and interface to the LLM, validates the resulting assets, and returns structured feedback. In this way, the LLM is not distracted by details such as authoring a URDF file or managing a complex software environment. We show that this produces higher-quality assets than both state-of-the-art articulated-asset generators and general-purpose coding agents. Using Articraft, we build Articraft-10K, a curated dataset of over 10K articulated assets spanning 245 categories, and show its utility both for training models of articulated assets and in downstream applications such as robotics simulation and virtual reality.

</details>


### [114] [RAVEN: Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation with Consistency-model GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15190)
*Yanzuo Lu,Ronglai Zuo,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RAVEN框架，通过训练时回滚重排解决因果自回归视频扩散模型中历史分布偏移问题，并引入CM-GRPO方法将一致性模型与强化学习直接结合，提升长时序视频生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果自回归视频扩散模型在训练和推理时面临历史分布不一致的问题，限制了长时序生成质量。现有方法未有效对齐训练注意力与推理外推过程。

Method: 1) RAVEN：训练时将模型的自回归回滚结果重排为干净历史端点与噪声去噪状态的交错序列，使训练注意力对齐推理外推，并允许下游块损失反向监督历史表征。2) CM-GRPO：将一致性采样步重写为条件高斯转移，直接在该核上应用在线强化学习，避免传统流模型RL中的Euler-Maruyama辅助过程。

Result: RAVEN在质量、语义和动态评估上超越现有因果视频蒸馏基线；CM-GRPO结合RAVEN可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 提出的RAVEN和CM-GRPO有效解决了自回归视频扩散中的分布偏移问题，并在因果视频生成任务上取得了SOTA结果。

Abstract: Causal autoregressive video diffusion models support real-time streaming generation by extrapolating future chunks from previously generated content. Distilling such generators from high-fidelity bidirectional teachers yields competitive few-step models, yet a persistent gap between the history distributions encountered during training and those arising at inference constrains generation quality over long horizons. We introduce the Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation Network (RAVEN), a training-time test framework that repacks each self rollout into an interleaved sequence of clean historical endpoints and noisy denoising states. This formulation aligns training attention with inference-time extrapolation and allows downstream chunk losses to supervise the history representations on which future predictions depend. We further propose Consistency-model Group Relative Policy Optimization (CM-GRPO), which reformulates a consistency sampling step as a conditional Gaussian transition and applies online Reinforcement Learning (RL) directly to this kernel, avoiding the Euler-Maruyama auxiliary process adopted in prior flow-model RL formulations. Experiments demonstrate that RAVEN surpasses recent causal video distillation baselines across quality, semantic, and dynamic degree evaluations, and that CM-GRPO provides further gains when combined with RAVEN.

</details>


### [115] [Aligning Latent Geometry for Spherical Flow Matching in Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15193)
*Tuna Han Salih Meral,Kaan Oktay,Hidir Yesiltepe,Adil Kaan Akan,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在球面上进行潜流匹配生成，将数据潜变量和噪声均投影到固定半径的球面上，并用球面线性插值替代线性插值，使得路径始终位于球面上，速度目标为纯角度变化，显著提升了类条件ImageNet-256的FID指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准潜流匹配中，高斯噪声和VAE潜变量都集中在薄球壳上，欧几里得弦会离开这些球壳，导致路径次优。作者发现潜变量的感知和语义内容主要由角度方向承载，半径贡献很小，因此希望通过球面投影消除半径维度的冗余信息。

Method: 1) 将每个潜变量token分解为径向和角度分量；2) 将数据潜变量投影到固定token半径上；3) 将高斯噪声的径向投影作为球形先验；4) 冻结编码器，微调解码器；5) 用球面线性插值替换线性插值，使得路径每步都在球面上，速度目标为纯角度变化。

Result: 在匹配训练条件下，该方法在不同图像分词器上一致改善了类条件ImageNet-256的FID指标，且不改变扩散架构，无需辅助编码器或表示对齐目标。

Conclusion: 球面流匹配是一种简单有效的改进，通过消除半径维度噪音、强制路径位于流形上来提升生成质量，且与现有扩散架构兼容。

Abstract: Latent flow matching for image generation usually transports Gaussian noise to variational autoencoder latents along linear paths. Both endpoints, however, concentrate in thin spherical shells, and a Euclidean chord leaves those shells even when preprocessing aligns their radii. By decomposing each latent token into radial and angular components, we show through component-swap probes that decoded perceptual and semantic content is carried predominantly by direction, with radius contributing much less. We therefore project data latents onto a fixed token radius, use the radial projection of Gaussian noise as the spherical prior, finetune the decoder with the encoder frozen, and replace linear interpolation with spherical linear interpolation. The resulting geodesic paths stay on the sphere at every timestep, and their velocity targets are purely angular by construction. Under matched training, the method consistently improves class-conditional ImageNet-256 FID across different image tokenizers, leaves the diffusion architecture unchanged, and requires no auxiliary encoder or representation-alignment objective.

</details>


### [116] [EntityBench: Towards Entity-Consistent Long-Range Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15199)
*Ruozhen He,Meng Wei,Ziyan Yang,Vicente Ordonez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EntityBench基准（140个片段/2491个镜头，来自真实叙事媒体），用于评估多镜头视频生成中的实体一致性，配套三支柱评估套件；同时提出EntityMem基线方法，通过持久化记忆库存储实体视觉参考，在跨镜头角色一致性上显著优于现有方法（Cohen's d = +2.33）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成评估通常使用独立生成的提示集，实体覆盖有限且一致性度量简单，缺乏标准化比较。多镜头视频生成在长序列中保持角色、物体和位置一致性仍具挑战。

Method: 1) EntityBench：从真实叙事媒体提取140个片段（2491个镜头），每个镜头标注实体调度表，分easy/medium/hard三档（最多50个镜头、13个跨镜头角色、8个跨镜头位置、22个跨镜头物体，重复间隔达48个镜头）。2) 三支柱评估套件：镜头内质量、提示对齐、跨镜头一致性，含保真门控机制。3) EntityMem：基于记忆增强的生成系统，在生成前将验证后的实体视觉参考存储到持久化记忆库中。

Result: 现有方法的跨镜头实体一致性随重复间隔增大而急剧下降；EntityMem显式逐实体记忆方法在所有评估方法中取得了最高的角色保真度（Cohen's d = +2.33）和出现率。

Conclusion: EntityBench为多镜头视频生成提供了标准化评估基准，EntityMem证明了显式记忆机制对提升跨镜头一致性的有效性，未来可进一步扩展实体类型和评估维度。

Abstract: Multi-shot video generation extends single-shot generation to coherent visual narratives, yet maintaining consistent characters, objects, and locations across shots remains a challenge over long sequences. Existing evaluations typically use independently generated prompt sets with limited entity coverage and simple consistency metrics, making standardized comparison difficult. We introduce EntityBench, a benchmark of 140 episodes (2,491 shots) derived from real narrative media, with explicit per-shot entity schedules tracking characters, objects, and locations simultaneously across easy / medium / hard tiers of up to 50 shots, 13 cross-shot characters, 8 cross-shot locations, 22 cross-shot objects, and recurrence gaps spanning up to 48 shots. It is paired with a three-pillar evaluation suite that disentangles intra-shot quality, prompt-following alignment, and cross-shot consistency, with a fidelity gate that admits only accurate entity appearances into cross-shot scoring. As a baseline, we propose EntityMem, a memory-augmented generation system that stores verified per-entity visual references in a persistent memory bank before generation begins. Experiments show that cross-shot entity consistency degrades sharply with recurrence distance in existing methods, and that explicit per-entity memory yields the highest character fidelity (Cohen's d = +2.33) and presence among methods evaluated. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Catherine-R-He/EntityBench/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Merging Methods for Multilingual Knowledge Editing for Large Language Models: An Empirical Odyssey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13919)
*Kunil Lee,Ki-Young Shin,Jong-Hyeok Lee,Young-Joo Suh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了向量合并方法在多语言知识编辑（MKE）中的有效性，发现带共享协方差的向量求和是最可靠的策略，而简单求和表现不佳；TSVM在部分场景下能提升性能但缓解多语言干扰的能力有限；性能和权重缩放因子与秩压缩比高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言知识编辑面临语言特定编辑之间的相互干扰问题，即便定位-编辑方法在单语场景表现良好，在多语言场景下仍存在挑战。本文旨在探究向量合并方法能否有效缓解这种多语言干扰。

Method: 在MzsRE基准上，使用两种主流大语言模型、两种基础知识编辑方法和12种语言，在大规模批量编辑场景下评估了六种合并变体，分析TSVM的效果以及权重缩放因子和秩压缩比对性能的影响。

Result: 带共享协方差的向量求和是最可靠的全局策略；简单求和性能差；TSVM在部分设置下可提升性能但缓解多语言干扰能力有限；性能对权重缩放因子和秩比敏感，更大的缩放因子和相对较低的秩通常效果更好。

Conclusion: 阐明了当前向量合并方法在多语言知识编辑中的实际优势和局限，为未来多语言知识编辑研究提供了指导。

Abstract: Multilingual knowledge editing (MKE) remains challenging because language-specific edits interfere with one another, even when locate-then-edit methods work well in monolingual settings. This paper focuses on three issues: the effectiveness of vector merging methods for MKE, the extent to which Task Singular Vectors for Merging (TSVM) can reduce multilingual interference, and the influence of the weight scaling factor and rank compression ratio on performance. We evaluate six merging variants with two popular backbone large language models, two base knowledge editing methods, and 12 languages on the MzsRE benchmark under a large-scale batch-editing setting. Our results show that vector summation with shared covariance is the most reliable overall strategy, whereas simple summation without shared covariance performs poorly. TSVM improves performance in some settings, but its ability to mitigate multilingual interference is limited. We also find that performance is sensitive to both weight scale and rank ratio, with larger-than-default scaling and relatively low rank often yielding better results. These findings clarify the practical strengths and limits of current vector merging methods for MKE and provide guidance for future multilingual knowledge editing research.

</details>


### [118] [Mistletoe: Stealthy Acceleration-Collapse Attacks on Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14005)
*Shuoyang Sun,Chang Da,Hao Fang,Kuofeng Gao,Xinhao Zhong,Yi Sun,Fan Mo,Shu-Tao Xia,Bin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现基于模型的推测解码中，由于草稿模型与目标模型之间的近似不完美，存在一种新的机制级漏洞。作者提出Mistletoe攻击方法，通过联合优化降低草稿-目标一致性的退化目标和保持语义的约束目标，并引入零空间投影机制解决两者冲突，从而在不影响输出质量的前提下显著降低推测解码的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码的效率关键依赖于平均接受长度 τ，而现有研究主要关注输出鲁棒性，忽略了推测解码机制本身存在的攻击面。本文旨在揭示草稿模型与目标模型之间不可避免的近似误差可被利用来攻击推测解码的加速性能。

Method: 提出Mistletoe攻击方法：(1) 联合优化一个降低草稿-目标一致性的退化目标和一个保持目标模型输出分布的语义保持目标；(2) 引入零空间投影机制，将退化梯度投影到偏离局部语义保持方向的方向上，从而在抑制草稿接受度的同时最小化语义漂移。

Result: 在多种推测解码系统上的实验表明，Mistletoe能显著降低平均接受长度 τ，破坏加速效果，降低平均令牌吞吐量，同时保持输出质量和困惑度。

Conclusion: 推测解码在现有输出鲁棒性之外引入了一个新的机制级攻击面，需要更鲁棒的LLM加速系统设计来应对此类威胁。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has become a widely adopted technique for accelerating large language model (LLM) inference by drafting multiple candidate tokens and verifying them with a target model in parallel. Its efficiency, however, critically depends on the average accepted length $τ$, i.e., how many draft tokens survive each verification step. In this work, we identify a new mechanism-level vulnerability in model-based speculative decoding: the drafter is trained to approximate the target model distribution, but this approximation is inevitably imperfect. Such a drafter-target mismatch creates a hidden attack surface where small perturbations can preserve the target model's visible behavior while substantially reducing draft-token acceptability. We propose Mistletoe, a stealthy acceleration-collapse attack against speculative decoding. Mistletoe directly targets the acceptance mechanism of speculative decoding. It jointly optimizes a degradation objective that decreases drafter-target agreement and a semantic-preservation objective that constrains the target model's output distribution. To resolve the conflict between these objectives, we introduce a null-space projection mechanism, where degradation gradients are projected away from the local semantic-preserving direction, suppressing draft acceptance while minimizing semantic drift. Experiments on various speculative decoding systems show that Mistletoe substantially reduces average accepted length $τ$, collapses speedup, and lowers averaged token throughput, while preserving output quality and perplexity. Our work highlights that speculative decoding introduces a mechanism-level attack surface beyond existing output robustness, calling for more robust designs of LLM acceleration systems.

</details>


### [119] [Physics-R1: An Audited Olympiad Corpus and Recipe for Visual Physics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14040)
*Shan Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了多模态物理评估流程，揭露了三种未被发现的构建缺陷（训练-评估污染、翻译漂移、多选题饱和），并发布四个新数据集和一个强化学习训练方案Physics-R1，将8B模型在物理推理任务上提升显著，部分指标超越32B模型和Gemini 2.5 Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态物理推理评估流程存在未被检测的系统性缺陷，导致模型在物理视觉-语言推理上的真实能力被高估，亟需进行端到端审计并构建更可靠的评估体系。

Method: 采用三阶段审计流程（Jaccard → mxbai-embed-large余弦相似度 → Haiku-4.5 LLM评判）检测训练-评估污染；通过Estonian-English双语对照实验测量翻译漂移；通过MCQ与开放式问答对比实验测量多选题饱和效应。基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking，采用GSPO+DAPO强化学习方案训练Physics-R1。

Result: 训练-评估污染：SciInstruct中发现134个近重复和4,846个释义候选；翻译漂移：Sonnet 4.5在爱沙尼亚-英语配对题目上表现差17个百分点（p=0.011）；MCQ饱和：相同模型在MCQ上79.7% vs 开放式33.4%（差距46个百分点）。Physics-R1在PhysOlym-A上提升18.3个百分点，在PhysReason上超越Qwen3-VL-32B和Gemini 2.5 Pro。

Conclusion: 当前多模态物理评估存在系统性问题，需要更严格的审计流程；Physics-R1证明了强化学习结合干净评估数据能有效提升小模型物理推理能力，发布的数据集和方案为社区提供了更可靠的评估基准。

Abstract: We audit the multimodal-physics evaluation pipeline end-to-end and document three undetected construction practices that distort how the field measures vision-language reasoning: train-eval contamination, translation drift, and MCQ saturation. (1) Public training pools (UGPhysics-Train, SciInstruct, MMK12) pass single-stage 5-gram-Jaccard audits with zero hits across all six public physics evals; a three-stage audit (Jaccard -> mxbai-embed-large cosine -> Haiku-4.5 LLM-judge) surfaces 134 near-duplicates and 4,846 paraphrase candidates in SciInstruct alone. (2) A 17-pp Sonnet 4.5 delta on 59 paired Estonian-English olympiad problems (30.5% vs. 13.6%; sign test p=0.011, McNemar p=0.021, paired bootstrap 95% CI [+5.1, +28.9] pp). (3) A 46-pp format-and-novelty gradient on identical Sonnet weights between MCQ (79.7% on PhyX) and open-ended olympiad evaluation (33.4% on PhysOlym-A). We release four artifacts addressing these gaps: PhysCorp-A (6,432-record three-stage-audited multimodal corpus), PhysR1Corp (2,268-record closed-form RL pool), PhysOlym-A (500-problem, 99.8% novel-source held-out olympiad eval with native difficulty labels and an EN/ET bilingual subset), and Physics-R1, a reference GSPO+DAPO recipe cold-started from Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking. Across 3 seeds, Physics-R1 lifts the audited corpus over the 8B base by +18.3 pp on PhysOlym-A liberal (8.0 -> 26.3 +/- 1.7; 7.1 pp behind Sonnet 4.5), +15.7 pp on PhysReason (23.9 -> 39.6 +/- 6.4; ahead of Qwen3-VL-32B and Gemini 2.5 Pro), +6.9 pp on OlympiadBench-Physics (46.2 +/- 1.5), and +4.1 pp on PhyX MCQ (77.8 +/- 0.3).

</details>


### [120] [Derivation Prompting: A Logic-Based Method for Improving Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14053)
*Ignacio Sastre,Guillermo Moncecchi,Aiala Rosá*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Derivation Prompting，一种受逻辑推导启发的RAG提示技术，通过构建可解释的推导树来控制生成过程，在领域特定QA任务中显著减少不可接受答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在知识密集型、领域特定的问答任务中容易出现幻觉和错误推理，现有RAG方法缺乏对生成过程的细粒度控制。

Method: 提出Derivation Prompting方法，从初始假设出发，通过系统应用预定义规则进行推导，构建可解释的推导树，从而增强RAG生成阶段的可控性和可解释性。

Result: 在特定案例研究中，该方法相较于传统RAG和长上下文窗口方法，显著减少了不可接受答案的比例。

Conclusion: Derivation Prompting通过引入逻辑推导机制，有效提升了RAG在领域特定任务中的生成质量，降低了错误输出。

Abstract: The application of Large Language Models to Question Answering has shown great promise, but important challenges such as hallucinations and erroneous reasoning arise when using these models, particularly in knowledge-intensive, domain-specific tasks. To address these issues, we introduce Derivation Prompting, a novel prompting technique for the generation step of the Retrieval-Augmented Generation framework. Inspired by logic derivations, this method involves deriving conclusions from initial hypotheses through the systematic application of predefined rules. It constructs a derivation tree that is interpretable and adds control over the generation process. We applied this method in a specific case study, significantly reducing unacceptable answers compared to traditional RAG and long-context window methods.

</details>


### [121] [PEML: Parameter-efficient Multi-Task Learning with Optimized Continuous Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14055)
*Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Syed Zawad,Xiaolong Ma,Xu Dong,Feng Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PEML方法，通过联合优化连续提示和低秩模型适配，实现参数高效的多任务微调，在多个基准上平均提升6.67%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法（如LoRA、Prefix Tuning）主要针对单任务设计，缺乏对提示优化和模型适配的协同优化，难以高效支持多任务学习。

Method: 提出PEML框架，采用神经架构工程方法优化连续提示（prompt优化），同时结合低秩适配（LoRA）调整模型权重，实现提示优化与模型适配的协同优化。

Result: 在GLUE、SuperGLUE、MMLU、常识推理基准上，平均准确率提升最高达6.67%，单任务峰值提升10.75%。

Conclusion: 提示优化与模型适配的协同优化是实现参数高效多任务学习的关键，PEML在多项任务上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) is widely used for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) for various tasks. Recently, there has been an increasing demand for fine-tuning a single LLM for multiple tasks because it requires overall less data for fine-tuning thanks to the common features shared among tasks. More importantly, LLMs are resource demanding and deploying a single model for multiple tasks facilitates resource consolidation and consumes significantly less resources compared to deploying individual large model for each task. Existing PEFT methods like LoRA and Prefix Tuning are designed to adapt LLMs to a specific task. LoRA and its variation focus on aligning the model itself for tasks, overlooking the importance of prompt tuning in multi-task learning while Prefix Tuning only adopts a simple architecture to optimize prompts, which limits the adaption capabilities for multi-task. To enable efficient fine-tuning for multi-task learning, it is important to co-optimize prompt optimization and model adaptation. In this work, we propose a Parameter-Efficient Multi-task Learning (\PM), which employs a neural architecture engineering method for optimizing the continuous prompts while also performing low-rank adaption for model weights. We prototype PEML by creating an automated framework for optimizing the continuous prompts and adapting model weights. We evaluate PEML against state-of-the-arts multi-task learning methods MTL-LoRA, MultiLoRa, C-Poly, and MoE, on the GLUE, SuperGLUE, Massive Multitask Language Understanding, and commonsense reasoning benchmarks. The evaluation results present an average accuracy improvement of up to 6.67%, with individual tasks showing peak gains of up to 10.75%.

</details>


### [122] [When Evidence Conflicts: Uncertainty and Order Effects in Retrieval-Augmented Biomedical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14115)
*Yikun Han,Mengfei Lan,Halil Kilicoglu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对生物医学检索增强大语言模型在矛盾证据下的可靠性问题，提出了冲突感知的弃权策略，证明仅靠置信度不足以应对证据冲突，而结合冲突检测的弃权评分能显著提升模型在困难场景下的选择性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学检索增强LLM评估通常只关注有帮助上下文下的答案准确性，忽视了证据可能不完整、误导或自相矛盾的情况，缺乏对矛盾证据下模型可靠性的系统评估和应对方法。

Method: 使用HealthContradict数据集，在五种受控证据条件下评估六个开源LLM：无检索上下文、仅正确上下文、仅错误上下文、以及两种包含正确和矛盾文档的混合条件（顺序相反）。提出一种结合模型置信度与证据冲突检测器的冲突感知弃权评分，用于支持困难情况下的弃权决策。

Result: 在矛盾证据顺序对比中，所有模型准确率均下降，11.4%-25.2%的预测发生翻转。冲突感知弃权评分在两种最困难条件下均优于仅依赖置信度的方法，在仅错误（IC）条件下平均提升7.2-33.4个点，在错误优先冲突（ICC）条件下平均提升3.6-14.4个点（覆盖率为75%、50%、25%时）。

Conclusion: 冲突性生物医学证据既是一个不确定性问题，也是一个鲁棒性问题，需要明确考虑证据不一致的评估和弃权方法。

Abstract: Biomedical retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs) often face evidence that is incomplete, misleading, or internally contradictory, yet evaluation usually emphasizes answer accuracy under helpful context rather than reliability under conflict. Using HealthContradict, we evaluate six open-weight LLMs under five controlled evidence conditions: no retrieved context, correct-only context, incorrect-only context, and two mixed conditions containing both correct and contradictory documents in opposite orders. In this conflicting-evidence order contrast, where the same two documents are both present and only their order is reversed, accuracy drops for every model and 11.4%--25.2% of predictions flip. To support abstention in these difficult cases, we also evaluate a conflict-aware abstention score that combines model confidence with a detector of evidence conflict. In the two hardest conditions, this score improves selective accuracy over confidence-only, with mean gains of 7.2--33.4 points in incorrect-only (`IC') and 3.6--14.4 points in incorrect-first conflicting (`ICC') conditions across 75%, 50%, and 25% coverage. These results show that conflicting biomedical evidence is both an uncertainty and robustness problem and motivate evaluation and abstention methods that explicitly account for evidence disagreement.

</details>


### [123] [Generative Floor Plan Design with LLMs via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14117)
*Luis Lara,Aristides Milios,Zhi Hao Luo,Aditya Sharma,Ge Ya Luo,Christopher Beckham,Florian Golemo,Christopher Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于文本的户型图生成方法，通过微调大语言模型并结合强化学习（RLVR），在满足拓扑连接和数值约束（如房间尺寸和面积）方面显著优于现有方法，兼容性指标相对提升至少94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成方法主要关注房间之间的拓扑连接关系，但无法支持对房间尺寸和面积等数值约束的精确控制，缺乏同时满足拓扑与数值约束的户型图生成方案。

Method: 微调大语言模型（LLM）于真实户型数据，然后应用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）来增强对拓扑和数值约束的遵从性，同时抑制无效或重叠输出；并设计了一套约束遵从度量指标来系统评估生成效果。

Result: 在真实性、兼容性和多样性指标上均优于现有方法；在所有任务中，兼容性指标的相对降低至少达到94%。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效处理户型图中的拓扑与数值约束，表明基于文本的生成建模在专业户型设计领域具有更广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: An AI system for professional floor plan design must precisely control room dimensions and areas while respecting the desired connectivity between rooms and maintaining functional and aesthetic quality. Existing generative approaches focus primarily on respecting the requested connectivity between rooms, but do not support generating floor plans that respect numerical constraints. We introduce a text-based floor plan generation approach that fine-tunes a large language model (LLM) on real plans and then applies reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) to improve adherence to topological and numerical constraints while discouraging invalid or overlapping outputs. Furthermore, we design a set of constraint adherence metrics to systematically measure how generated floor plans align with user-defined constraints. Our model generates floor plans that satisfy user-defined connectivity and numerical constraints and outperforms existing methods on Realism, Compatibility, and Diversity metrics. Across all tasks, our approach achieves at least a 94% relative reduction in Compatibility compared with existing methods. Our results demonstrate that LLMs can effectively handle constraints in this setting, suggesting broader applications for text-based generative modeling.

</details>


### [124] [ROK-FORTRESS: Measuring the Effect of Geopolitical Transcreation for National Security and Public Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14152)
*Michael S. Lee,Yash Maurya,Drew Rein,Bert Herring,Jonathan Nguyen,Kyungho Song,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Jiyeon Cho,Kaustubh Deshpande,Yeongkyun Jang,Jiyeon Joo,Minn Seok Choi,Evi Fuelle,Christina Q Knight,Joseph Brandifino,Max Fenkell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ROK-FORTRESS，一个英韩双语、文化对抗性的NSPS基准，通过“转创矩阵”分离语言和地缘政治语境的影响。实验发现韩语变体存在一致的抑制效应，且翻译-only评估会遗漏语言-风险信号与语境交互的关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评估在多语言场景中仅通过翻译基准测试，忽略了语言与地缘政治语境之间的交互作用，且实证证据局限于少数语言对。

Method: 构建ROK-FORTRESS英韩双语NSPS基准，采用“转创矩阵”设计：在2（英语vs韩语）×2（美国vs韩国实体/机构/操作细节）的受控组合下评估对抗性意图，每个对抗提示配对一个双重用途良性对照以量化过度拒绝，使用校准的LLM-as-a-judge和专家制定的二元评分标准。

Result: 韩语变体存在一致的抑制效应；地缘政治语境与语言的交互存在显著的模型间差异；多数模型中韩国语境可缓解韩语驱动的抑制，无模型出现反向显著放大；翻译-only评估会遗漏这些交互效应。

Conclusion: 语言作为风险信号的效应与语境交互对安全行为有显著影响，转创矩阵方法可推广至其他语言-文化对。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for large language models (LLMs) increasingly target high-stakes National Security and Public Safety (NSPS) risks, yet multilingual safety is typically assessed through translation-only benchmarks that preserve the underlying scenario, and empirical evidence of how language and geopolitical context interact remains limited to a narrow set of language pairs. We introduce \emph{ROK-FORTRESS} https://huggingface.co/datasets/ScaleAI/ROK-FORTRESS_public, a bilingual, culturally adversarial NSPS benchmark that uses the English--Korean language pair and U.S.--ROK geopolitical axis as a case study, separating the effects of language and geopolitical grounding via a \emph{transcreation matrix}: adversarial intents are evaluated under controlled combinations of (i) English versus Korean language and (ii) U.S.\ versus Korean entities, institutions, and operational details. Each adversarial prompt is paired with a dual-use benign counterpart to quantify over-refusal. Model responses are then scored using calibrated LLM-as-a-judge panels, applying our expert-crafted, prompt-specific binary rubrics.
  Across a dual-track set of frontier and Korean-optimized models, we find a consistent suppression effect in Korean variants and substantial model-to-model variation in how geopolitical grounding interacts with language. In many models, Korean grounding mitigates the Korean language-driven suppression -- with no model showing significant amplification in the other direction -- indicating that, at least in the English--Korean case, safety behavior is shaped by language-as-risk signals and context interactions that translation-only evaluations miss. The transcreation matrix methodology is designed to generalize to other language--culture pairs.

</details>


### [125] [Why Retrieval-Augmented Generation Fails: A Graph Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14192)
*Kai Guo,Xinnan Dai,Zhibo Zhang,Nuohan Lin,Shenglai Zeng,Jie Ren,Haoyu Han,Jiliang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过电路追踪方法构建归因图，研究RAG系统中检索证据如何影响答案生成。发现正确预测具有更深推理路径、更分布式证据流和更结构化局部连接模式，而失败预测则呈现浅层、碎片化和过度集中的证据流。基于此，开发了图拓扑特征的错误检测框架，并通过干预重塑内部路由以减少错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RAG系统已被广泛用于提升大语言模型，但其在拥有外部信息的情况下仍会产生错误答案，根本原因尚不清楚。现有研究缺乏对检索证据如何被模型内部整合的细粒度理解。

Method: 采用电路追踪技术构建归因图，建模Transformer解码过程中检索上下文、中间激活和生成token之间的信息流，从图结构层面分析证据整合模式，并基于拓扑特征设计错误检测与干预方法。

Result: 正确与错误预测在归因图拓扑结构上存在一致差异：正确预测具有更深推理路径、更分布式证据流和更结构化局部连接；错误预测则表现出浅层、碎片化和过度集中的证据流。

Conclusion: 归因图拓扑特征可用于检测RAG错误，且通过强化问题约束的证据接地干预能够重塑内部路由，提升检索信息整合效果并减少错误。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a powerful and widely used approach for improving large language models by grounding generation in retrieved evidence. However, RAG systems still produce incorrect answers in many cases. Why RAG fails despite having access to external information remains poorly understood. We present a model-internal study of retrieval-augmented generation that examines how retrieved evidence influences answer generation. Using circuit tracing, we construct attribution graphs that model the flow of information through transformer layers during decoding. These graphs represent interactions among retrieved context, intermediate model activations, and generated tokens, providing a graph, circuit-level view of how external evidence is integrated into the model's reasoning process across multiple question answering benchmarks, we observe consistent structural differences: correct predictions exhibit deeper reasoning paths, more distributed evidence flow, and a more structured pattern of local connectivity, while failed predictions show shallower, fragmented, and overly concentrated evidence flow. Building on these findings, we develop a graph-based error detection framework that uses attribution-graph topology features. Furthermore, we show that attribution graphs enable targeted interventions. By reinforcing question-constrained evidence grounding, we reshape internal routing so that answer generation remains guided by the question, leading to more effective integration of retrieved information and fewer errors.

</details>


### [126] [GradShield: Alignment Preserving Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14194)
*Zhanhao Hu,Xiao Huang,Patrick Mendoza,Emad A. Alghamdi,Basel Alomair,Raluca Ada Popa,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GradShield，一种在微调过程中通过计算隐式危害分数（FIHS）并采用自适应阈值算法过滤有害数据，从而保护LLM安全对齐的过滤方法。实验表明，该方法在保持模型效用性能的同时，将攻击成功率（ASR）稳定控制在6%以下，优于所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在微调后存在严重的安全对齐风险，因为显式和隐式有害数据都可能破坏模型的对齐状态，甚至一些看似良性的数据也会无意中引导模型产生不当行为。

Method: 提出GradShield方法：为每个数据点计算微调隐式危害分数（FIHS），并采用自适应阈值算法识别和过滤有害数据点，在微调前将其移除，从而保护模型的安全对齐。

Result: 在多种实用性微调任务和不同有害数据比例下，GradShield在所有评估指标上均优于所有基线方法，能够将攻击成功率（ASR）稳定维持在6%以下，同时不影响模型的效用性能。

Conclusion: GradShield是一种有效的LLM微调安全防护方法，能够在不牺牲模型性能的前提下，显著降低微调过程中的安全风险。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) pose a significant risk of safety misalignment after finetuning, as models can be compromised by both explicitly and implicitly harmful data. Even some seemingly benign data can inadvertently steer a model towards misaligned behaviors. To address this, we introduce GradShield, a principled filtering method that safeguards LLMs during finetuning by identifying and removing harmful data points before they corrupt the model's alignment. It removes potentially harmful data by computing a Finetuning Implicit Harmfulness Score (FIHS) for each data point and employs an adaptive thresholding algorithm. We apply GradShield to multiple utility fine-tuning tasks across varying levels of harmful data and evaluate the safety and utility performance of the resulting LLMs using various metrics. The results show that GradShield outperforms all baseline methods, consistently maintaining an Attack Success Rate (ASR) below $6\%$ while preserving utility performance.

</details>


### [127] [What Makes Words Hard? Sakura at BEA 2026 Shared Task on Vocabulary Difficulty Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14257)
*Adam Nohejl,Xuanxin Wu,Yusuke Ide,Maria Angelica Riera Machin,Yi-Ning Chang,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了两种词汇难度预测模型：高精度黑盒模型（在公开赛道取得第一）和可解释模型。黑盒模型通过软目标损失函数微调LLM，达到r>0.91；可解释模型在保持r>0.77相关性的同时提供了难度影响因素的解释。分析表明British Council的KVL词汇表中，单词难度常受拼写难度或测试题项构造影响，而非仅由词汇本身的生产难度决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有词汇难度预测模型要么精度高但缺乏可解释性，要么可解释但精度不足。作者希望同时实现高精度预测和可解释性，以更好地理解词汇难度的影响因素。

Method: 1）黑盒模型：采用软目标损失函数（soft-target loss function）微调大语言模型（LLM），用于评分任务。2）可解释模型：在保持可解释性的同时优于微调编码器基线。实验基于British Council的Knowledge-based Vocabulary Lists (KVL)数据集。

Result: 黑盒模型在公开赛道取得第一，相关系数r>0.91；可解释模型相关系数r>0.77，优于微调编码器基线。分析发现KVL词汇表的难度受拼写难度和测试题项构造影响，而非纯粹由词汇生产难度决定。

Conclusion: 软目标损失函数微调LLM能有效提升词汇难度预测精度；可解释模型在解释性和性能之间取得了良好平衡；词汇难度评估需考虑拼写和题项构造等额外因素；代码已开源。

Abstract: We describe two types of models for vocabulary difficulty prediction: a high-accuracy black-box model, which achieved the top shared task result in the open track, and an explainable model, which outperforms a fine-tuned encoder baseline. As the black-box model, we fine-tuned an LLM using a soft-target loss function for effective application to the rating task, achieving r > 0.91. The explainable model provides insights into what impacts the difficulty of each item while maintaining a strong correlation (r > 0.77). We further analyze the results, demonstrating that the difficulty of items in the British Council's Knowledge-based Vocabulary Lists (KVL) is often affected by spelling difficulty or the construction of the test items, in addition to the genuine production difficulty of the words. We make our code available online at https://github.com/adno/vocabulary-difficulty .

</details>


### [128] [Auditing Agent Harness Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14271)
*Chengzhi Liu,Yichen Guo,Yepeng Liu,Yuzhe Yang,Qianqi Yan,Xuandong Zhao,Wenyue Hua,Sheng Liu,Sharon Li,Yuheng Bu,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了HarnessAudit框架，用于审计LLM agents执行全轨迹的边界合规、执行忠实性和系统稳定性，并构建了包含210个任务的HarnessAudit-Bench基准。实验发现任务完成与安全执行不一致，违规随轨迹长度累积，违规主要集中在资源访问和智能体间信息传递，多智能体协作扩大了安全风险面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准仅评估最终输出或终止状态，无法检测执行中间轨迹中的违规行为（如未授权资源访问、上下文泄露），而许多违规发生在轨迹中期而非终止时刻。

Method: 提出HarnessAudit框架，审计完整执行轨迹的边界合规性、执行忠实性和系统稳定性；构建HarnessAudit-Bench基准（210个任务，8个真实域，单智能体和多智能体配置）；评估10种承载配置下的前沿模型和3个多智能体框架。

Result: (i)任务完成与安全执行不一致，违规随轨迹长度累积；(ii)安全风险因域、任务类型和智能体角色而异；(iii)违规集中在资源访问和智能体间信息传递；(iv)多智能体协作扩大安全风险面，承载设计设定了安全部署的上限。

Conclusion: 承载执行系统需要完整轨迹审计，仅依靠输出级评估不足以保障安全性；承载设计是安全部署的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly run inside execution harnesses that dispatch tools, allocate resources, and route messages between specialized components. However, a harness can return a correct, benign answer over a trajectory that accesses unauthorized resources or leaks context to the wrong agent. Output-level evaluation cannot see these failures, yet most safety benchmarks score only final outputs or terminal states, even though many violations occur mid-trajectory rather than at termination. The central question is whether the harness respects user intent, permission boundaries, and information-flow constraints throughout execution. To address this gap, we propose HarnessAudit, a framework that audits full execution trajectories across boundary compliance, execution fidelity, and system stability, with a focus on multi-agent harnesses where these risks are most pronounced. We further introduce HarnessAudit-Bench, a benchmark of 210 tasks across eight real-world domains, instantiated in both single-agent and multi-agent configurations with embedded safety constraints. Evaluating ten harness configurations across frontier models and three multi-agent frameworks, we find that: (i) task completion is misaligned with safe execution, and violations accumulate with trajectory length; (ii) safety risks vary across domains, task types, and agent roles; (iii) most violations concentrate in resource access and inter-agent information transfer; and (iv) multi-agent collaboration expands the safety risk surface, while harness design sets the upper bound of safe deployment.

</details>


### [129] [Ideology Prediction of German Political Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14352)
*Sinclair Schneider,Florian Steuber,Joao A. G. Schneider,Gabi Dreo Rodosek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer的模型，可将德文文本投射到连续政治光谱（-1至1），使用四种语料库训练和测试，发现DeBERTa-large在域内表现最佳（F1=0.844），Gemma2-2B在域外新闻测试中表现最优（MAE=0.172），表明模型架构和领域训练数据对政治偏见估计的影响不亚于模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在连续左右光谱上精确投影政治文本取向，多类别分类器只能处理离散预定义类别，无法灵活聚焦特定政治光谱段（如仅关注保守派而排除自由派和极右翼）。

Method: 构建四种德文语料库：德国联邦议院会议记录、Wahl-O-Mat决策工具数据、33家报纸文章、597名议员Twitter数据。从13种候选Transformer中选择最优，使用两个语料库训练、两个测试，对比DeBERTa-large、Gemma2-2B等模型在域内和域外场景下的F1、ACC和MAE指标。

Result: DeBERTa-large在域内测试取得最高F1=0.844；在Twitter域外测试中ACC=0.864；Gemma2-2B在报纸域外测试中MAE=0.172最优。

Conclusion: Transformer模型可在德文新闻中识别政治框架，达到公众舆论调查水平；模型架构和领域训练数据的可用性对政治偏见估计的影响与模型规模同等重要；讨论了方法论局限和改进方向。

Abstract: Elections represent a crucial milestone in a nation's ongoing development. To better understand the political rhetoric from various movements, ranging from left to right, we propose a transformer-based model capable of projecting the political orientation of a text on a continuous left-to-right spectrum, represented by a normalized scalar d between -1 and 1. This approach enables analysts to focus on specific segments of the political landscape, such as conservatives, while excluding liberal and far-right movements. Such a task can only be achieved with multiclass classifiers, provided that the desired orientation is incorporated within one of their predefined classes. To determine the most suitable foundation model among 13 candidate transformers for this task, we constructed four distinct corpora. One corpus comprised annotated plenary notes from the German Bundestag, while another was based on an official online decision-making tool, Wahl-O-Mat. The third corpus consisted of articles from 33 newspapers, each identified by its political orientation, and the fourth included 535,200 tweets from 597 members of the 20th and 21st German Bundestag. To mitigate overfitting, we used two distinct corpora for training and two for testing, respectively. For in-domain performance, DeBERTa-large achieved the highest F1 score F1=0.844 as well as for the X (Twitter) out-of-domain test ACC=0.864. Regarding the newspaper out-of-domain test, Gemma2-2B excelled (MAE = 0.172). This study demonstrates that transformer models can recognize political framing in German news at the level of public opinion polls. Our findings suggest that both the model architecture and the availability of domain-specific training data can be as influential as model size for estimating political bias. We discuss methodological limitations and outline directions for improving the robustness of bias measurement.

</details>


### [130] [LLM-based Detection of Manipulative Political Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14354)
*Sinclair Schneider,Florian Steuber,Gabi Dreo Rodosek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合少量样本提示过滤与无监督聚类（UMAP+HDBSCAN）的计算框架，用于从超120万条社交媒体帖子中检测并结构化41个操纵性政治叙事集群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于政治讨论转向社交媒体，区分操纵性政治叙事与合法批评成为重要挑战，现有方法难以自动发现未知叙事类别。

Method: 1) 使用详细少量样本提示（few-shot prompt）结合推理模型过滤操纵性帖子；2) 对保留帖子进行嵌入并使用UMAP降维；3) 应用HDBSCAN无监督聚类发现叙事组；4) 使用推理模型揭示每个集群背后的叙事。

Result: 该方法应用于超过120万条社交媒体帖子，成功识别出41个不同的操纵性叙事集群。

Conclusion: 提示过滤与无监督聚类的集成框架能有效发现并结构化操纵性政治叙事，且无需预定义目标类别列表，具备发现新叙事集群的能力。

Abstract: We present a new computational framework for detecting and structuring manipulative political narratives. A task that became more important due to the shift of political discussions to social media. One of the primary challenges thereby is differentiating between manipulative political narratives and legitimate critiques. Some posts may also reframe actual events within a manipulative context.
  To achieve good clustering results, we filter manipulative posts beforehand using a detailed few-shot prompt that combines documented campaign narratives with legitimate criticisms to differentiate them. This prompt enables a reasoning model to assign labels, retaining only manipulative narrative posts for further processing.
  The remaining posts are subsequently embedded and dimensionality-reduced using UMAP, before HDBSCAN is applied to uncover narrative groups. A key advantage of this unsupervised approach is its independence from a predefined list of target categories, enabling it to uncover new narrative clusters.
  Finally, a reasoning model is employed to uncover the narrative behind each cluster. This approach, applied to over 1.2 million social media posts, effectively identified 41 distinct manipulative narrative clusters by integrating prompt-based filtering with unsupervised clustering.

</details>


### [131] [Reinforcement Learning with Semantic Rewards Enables Low-Resource Language Expansion without Alignment Tax](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14366)
*Zeli Su,Ziyin Zhang,Zhou Liu,Xuexian Song,Zhankai Xu,Longfei Zheng,Xiaolu Zhang,Rong Fu,Guixian Xu,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语义空间对齐的GRPO强化学习方法，替代传统SFT，在低资源语言扩展中避免"对齐税"，通过嵌入级语义奖励而非似然最大化优化模型，在藏汉翻译和标题生成任务上保持通用能力的同时有效获取低资源语言能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展LLM到低资源语言时会遇到"对齐税"问题：目标语言能力提升以通用能力灾难性遗忘为代价。作者认为这是SFT在狭窄有偏数据分布上进行词级表面模仿所导致的刚性限制。

Method: 提出语义空间对齐范式，采用GRPO强化学习，使用嵌入级语义奖励（而非似然最大化）优化模型。该目标鼓励通过灵活表达实现意义保留，实现受控更新，减少对预训练知识的破坏性干扰。

Result: 在藏汉机器翻译和藏语标题生成任务上，该方法在获取低资源能力的同时显著缓解了对齐税，比SFT更有效地保持了通用能力。虽表面重叠度较低，但语义RL在开放式生成中产生更高的语义质量和偏好，少样本迁移结果也表明学习到了更可迁移和鲁棒的表示。

Conclusion: 研究表明，带有语义奖励的强化学习为包容性低资源语言扩展提供了一条更安全、更可靠的路径。

Abstract: Extending large language models (LLMs) to low-resource languages often incurs an "alignment tax": improvements in the target language come at the cost of catastrophic forgetting in general capabilities. We argue that this trade-off arises from the rigidity of supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which enforces token-level surface imitation on narrow and biased data distributions. To address this limitation, we propose a semantic-space alignment paradigm powered by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), where the model is optimized using embedding-level semantic rewards rather than likelihood maximization. This objective encourages meaning preservation through flexible realizations, enabling controlled updates that reduce destructive interference with pretrained knowledge. We evaluate our approach on Tibetan-Chinese machine translation and Tibetan headline generation. Experiments show that our method acquires low-resource capabilities while markedly mitigating alignment tax, preserving general competence more effectively than SFT. Despite producing less rigid surface overlap, semantic RL yields higher semantic quality and preference in open-ended generation, and few-shot transfer results indicate that it learns more transferable and robust representations under limited supervision. Overall, our study demonstrates that reinforcement learning with semantic rewards provides a safer and more reliable pathway for inclusive low-resource language expansion.

</details>


### [132] [Mitigating Data Scarcity in Psychological Defense Classification with Context-Aware Synthetic Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14380)
*Hoang-Thuy-Duong Vu,Quoc-Cuong Pham,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对心理防御机制（PDMs）文本分类中的数据稀缺和类别不平衡问题，提出了一种结合上下文感知的合成增强框架与混合分类模型（CASA-PDC），在PsyDefDetect共享任务中达到58.26%准确率和24.62%宏F1，相比基线分别提升40.25%和15.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理防御机制（PDMs）的自动分类具有重要临床价值，但面临数据稀缺和类别不平衡两大挑战，而单纯依靠生成式增强方法无法解决缺乏心理学理论支撑的问题。

Method: 提出上下文感知的合成增强框架，结合混合分类模型——该模型整合上下文语言表示与基础临床特征，并利用150个标注防御条目；通过定义质量的提示来控制生成保真度。

Result: 在PsyDefDetect共享任务中，准确率58.26%（较DMRS Co-Pilot提升40.25%），宏F1 24.62%（提升15.99%）。

Conclusion: 证明了定义质量的提示直接决定生成保真度和下游性能，为低资源场景下基于心理学理论的防御机制分类建立了强基线。

Abstract: Psychological defense mechanisms (PDMs) are unconscious cognitive processes that modulate how individuals perceive and respond to emotional distress. Automatically classifying PDMs from text is clinically valuable but severely hindered by data scarcity and class imbalance, challenges which generative augmentation alone cannot resolve without psychological grounding. In this work, we address these challenges in the PsyDefDetect shared task (BioNLP@ACL 2026) by proposing a context-aware synthetic augmentation framework combined with a hybrid classification model. Our hybrid model integrates contextual language representations with basic clinical features, along with 150 annotated defense items. Experiments demonstrate that definition quality in prompting directly governs generation fidelity and downstream performance. Our method surpasses DMRS Co-Pilot, reaching an accuracy of 58.26% (+40.25%) and a macro-F1 of 24.62% (+15.99%), thereby establishing a strong baseline for psychologically grounded defense mechanism classification in low-resource settings. Source code is available at: https://github.com/htdgv/CASA-PDC.

</details>


### [133] [Agentic Recommender System with Hierarchical Belief-State Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14401)
*Xiang Shen,Yuhang Zhou,Yifan Wu,Zhuokai Zhao,Siyu Lin,Lei Huang,Qianqian Zhong,Lizhu Zhang,Benyu Zhang,Xiangjun Fan,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MARS是一个将推荐系统视为部分可观测问题、采用三层结构信念状态（事件记忆、偏好记忆、画像记忆）并配备六种生命周期操作（提取、强化、削弱、合并、遗忘、重构）的LLM智能体框架，通过LLM规划器自适应调度而非固定间隔启发式策略，在四个基准数据集上实现了HR@1提升26.4%、NDCG@10提升10.3%的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型LLM智能体采用扁平记忆表示，将瞬时信号与稳定偏好混为一谈，且缺乏完整的记忆生命周期管理机制，无法有效处理推荐场景中噪声观测与真实偏好之间的区分问题。

Method: 提出MARS框架，将推荐建模为部分可观测问题，维护结构化信念状态：事件记忆缓冲原始信号、偏好记忆以带强度和证据追踪的可变粒度块表示、画像记忆将偏好蒸馏为自然语言叙事；设计六种生命周期操作并由LLM规划器自适应调度。

Result: 在InstructRec四个基准域上达到SOTA，HR@1平均提升26.4%，NDCG@10平均提升10.3%，动态场景下智能体调度带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: 结构化分层记忆与自适应生命周期管理能够有效提升LLM推荐智能体的性能，特别是在处理噪声信号和偏好演化方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents have advanced personalized recommendation, yet existing approaches universally adopt flat memory representations that conflate ephemeral signals with stable preferences, and none provides a complete lifecycle governing how memory should evolve. We propose MARS (Memory-Augmented Agentic Recommender System), a framework that treats recommendation as a partially observable problem and maintains a structured belief state that progressively abstracts noisy behavioral observations into a compact estimate of user preferences. MARS organizes this belief state into three tiers: event memory buffers raw signals, preference memory maintains fine-grained mutable chunks with explicit strength and evidence tracking, and profile memory distills all preferences into a coherent natural language narrative. A complete lifecycle of six operations -- extraction, reinforcement, weakening, consolidation, forgetting, and resynthesis -- is adaptively scheduled by an LLM-based planner rather than fixed-interval heuristics. Experiments on four InstructRec benchmark domains show that \ours achieves state-of-the-art performance with average improvements of 26.4% in HR@1 and 10.3% in NDCG@10 over the strongest baselines with further gains from agentic scheduling in evolving settings.

</details>


### [134] [Knowledge Beyond Language: Bridging the Gap in Multilingual Machine Unlearning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14404)
*Kyomin Hwang,Hyeonjin Kim,Sangyeon Cho,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对多语言大模型中的机器遗忘（MMU）问题，提出两个新评估指标——知识可分离性得分（KSS）和知识持久性得分（KPS），分别衡量跨语言遗忘质量和跨语言信息一致性移除效果，为MMU评估提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMU评估方法仅是对单语言评估协议的简单扩展，未能捕捉信息在多语言间的跨语言分布特性，无法有效评估多语言环境下的遗忘效果。

Method: 提出KSS（评估跨语言整体遗忘质量）和KPS（评估不同语言对间信息移除的一致性）两个新指标，并在多语言设定下对多种遗忘方法进行综合评估分析。

Result: 通过新指标对多种遗忘方法的评估，揭示了MMU独有的现象，提供了新的评估视角。

Conclusion: KSS和KPS能够有效捕捉跨语言信息分布特性，为MMU评估提供了更全面、精准的度量标准，有助于推动多语言模型隐私保护研究。

Abstract: While LLMs are increasingly used in commercial services, they pose privacy risks such as leakage of sensitive personally identifiable information (PII). For LLMs trained on multilingual corpora, Multilingual Machine Unlearning (MMU) aims to remove information across multiple languages. However, prior MMU evaluations fail to capture such cross-linguistic distribution of information, being largely limited to direct extensions of per-language evaluation protocols. To this end, we propose two metrics to evaluate the information spread across languages: the Knowledge Separability Score (KSS) and the Knowledge Persistence Score (KPS). KSS measures the overall unlearning quality across multiple languages, while KPS more specifically aims to assess consistent removal of information among different language pairs. We evaluated various unlearning methods in the multilingual setting with these metrics and conducted comprehensive analyses. Through our investigation, we provide insights into unique phenomena exclusive to MMU and offer a new perspective on MMU evaluation.

</details>


### [135] [A Calculus-Based Framework for Determining Vocabulary Size in End-to-End ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14427)
*Sunil Kumar Kopparapu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于曲线拟合和微积分一阶/二阶导数检验的形式化方法，用于估计端到端ASR系统中最优的词表大小超参数，并在Librispeech语料库上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端ASR系统中词表大小是关键超参数，但现有工具包（如ESPNet）仅提供固定值，缺乏系统确定方法；虽有工作[1]提出了代价函数框架，但仍需更形式化的估计方法。

Method: 通过对训练数据进行曲线拟合，并运用微积分中的一阶和二阶导数检验，形式化地估计最优词表大小超参数。

Result: 在Librispeech语料库上的实验表明，该方法选出的最优词表大小能够提升ASR系统的性能。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于形式化了端到端ASR系统中最优词表大小的确定方法，填补了该超参数选择缺乏理论指导的空白。

Abstract: In hybrid automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems, the vocabulary size is unambiguous, typically determined by the number of phones, bi-phones, or tri-phones present in the language. In contrast, end-to-end ASR systems derive their vocabulary, often referred to as tokens from the text corpus used for training. The choice and, more importantly, the size of this vocabulary is a critical hyper-parameter in training end-to-end ASR systems. Tokenization algorithms such as Byte Pair Encoding (BPE), WordPiece, and Unigram Language Model (ULM) use the vocabulary size as an input hyper-parameter to generate the sub-words employed during ASR training. Popular toolkits like ESPNet provide a fixed vocabulary size in their training recipes, but there is little documentation or discussion in the literature regarding how these values are determined. Recent work [1] has formalized an approach to identify the vocabulary size best suited for end-to-end ASR, introducing a cost function framework that treats the tokenization process as a black box. In this paper, we build upon that foundation by curve fitting the training data and using the principle of first and second derivative tests in calculus to formally estimate the vocabulary size hyper-parameter. We demonstrate the utility and usefulness of our approach by applying it on a standard Librispeech corpus and show that the optimal choice of vocabulary size hyper-parameter improves the performance of the ASR. The main contribution of this paper in formalizing an approach to identify the vocabulary size best suited for training an end-to-end ASR system.

</details>


### [136] [Does RAG Know When Retrieval Is Wrong? Diagnosing Context Compliance under Knowledge Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14473)
*Yihang Chen,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Sipeng Zhang,Huan Xu,Shuhuai Lin,Xinpeng Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Context-Driven Decomposition (CDD)，一种推理时的信念分解探测与干预机制，用于分析和控制检索增强生成（RAG）中检索上下文与模型参数知识冲突时的"上下文服从"问题。实验表明CDD能有效暴露并改善标准RAG在对抗性设置下的鲁棒性，但跨模型家族的效果传递存在机制差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG评估仅关注准确率，无法揭示检索上下文与模型参数知识冲突时上下文如何因果性地塑造最终答案。需要一种机制来探测并干预这种"上下文服从"现象。

Method: 提出Context-Driven Decomposition (CDD)，一种在推理时运行的信念分解探测方法，同时作为受控检索冲突的干预机制，通过分解模型信念来检测和修正上下文主导。

Result: (P1) 标准RAG在TruthfulQA错误注入对抗设置下准确率仅15.0%。(P2) CDD提升了Gemini-2.5-Flash和Claude系列（Haiku/Sonnet/Opus）的准确率，但因果敏感性跨模型不迁移：Gemini达64.1%，Claude系列仅[-3%, +7%]。(P3) CDD在时间漂移(71.3%)和噪声干扰(69.9%)上显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 上下文服从是标准RAG中独立于检索质量和单一方法鲁棒性的结构性轴线，可通过CDD进行探测和干预。释放Epi-Scale基准以促进跨模型家族和检索管线的系统研究。

Abstract: The Context-Compliance Regime in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) occurs when retrieved context dominates
  the final answer even when it conflicts with the model's parametric knowledge. Accuracy alone does not reveal
  how retrieved context causally shapes answers under such conflict. We introduce Context-Driven Decomposition
  (CDD), a belief-decomposition probe that operates at inference time and serves as an intervention mechanism for
  controlled retrieval conflict. Across Epi-Scale stress tests, TruthfulQA misconception injection, and cross-
  model reruns, CDD exposes three patterns. P1: context compliance is measurable in an upper-bound adversarial
  setting, where Standard RAG reaches 15.0% accuracy on TruthfulQA misconception injection (N=500). P2:
  adversarial accuracy gains transfer across model families: CDD improves accuracy on Gemini-2.5-Flash and on
  Claude Haiku/Sonnet/Opus, but rationale-answer causal coupling does not transfer. CDD reaches 64.1% mistake-
  injection causal sensitivity on Gemini-2.5-Flash, while sensitivities for all three Claude variants fall in the
  [-3%, +7%] range, suggesting that the Claude-side accuracy gains operate through a mechanism distinct from the
  explicit conflict-resolution trace. P3: explicit conflict decomposition improves robustness under temporal
  drift and noisy distractors, with CDD reaching 71.3% on temporal shifts and 69.9% on distractor evidence on the
  full Epi-Scale adversarial benchmark. These three patterns identify context-compliance as a structural axis
  along which standard RAG can be probed and intervened on, distinct from retrieval-quality or single-method
  robustness questions, and motivate releasing Epi-Scale for systematic study across model families and retrieval
  pipelines.

</details>


### [137] [Cross-Linguistic Transcription and Phonological Representation in the Huìtóngguǎnxì Huáyíyìyǔ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14480)
*Ji-eun Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了15-16世纪明朝编纂的多语词汇集《会同馆华夷译语》（HHY）的音译原理，揭示其并非孤立语言材料的简单汇编，而是一个有内部结构的多语种音译系统，通过汉字来记录非汉语的口语形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究常将HHY的各语言部分视为独立材料，缺乏整体视角。本研究旨在证明HHY是一个系统的音译体系，而非零散材料集合，从而为历史音韵学尤其是记录匮乏的亚洲语言研究提供新证据。

Method: 将HHY大量内容数字化并与汉语音韵范畴对齐；批判性回顾各语言部分的前人构拟，整合为统一的比较数据库；重点分析八个语言部分中主音译（MT）和补音译（ST）的跨语言规律。

Result: 主音译（MT）主要表示与当时汉语音节结构兼容的语音；补音译（ST）主要编码与汉语音韵不太兼容的语音特征。汉语音韵范畴在外语音译中的运用比以往认为的更为灵活。HHY是一个相对系统的语音近似方法，而非汉语音韵直接投射到非汉语。

Conclusion: HHY可被视为一个有内部结构的音译系统而非简单汇编；历史音译系统可为历史音韵学提供重要证据，尤其有助于研究历史记录有限的亚洲语言。

Abstract: Purpose: This study investigates the transcription principles underlying Huìtóngguǎnxì Huáyíyìyǔ (HHY), a series of multilingual glossaries compiled by the Ming government between the fifteenth and sixteenth centuries for interpreter training. The study treats HHY not as a collection of isolated language materials, but as a coherent multilingual transcription system representing spoken forms of non-Chinese languages through Chinese characters.
  Methods: A substantial portion of HHY was digitized and aligned with Chinese phonological categories. Previous reconstructions of individual language sections were critically reviewed and integrated into a unified comparative database. The analysis focuses on cross-linguistic regularities in Main Transcription (MT) and Supplementary Transcription (ST) across eight language sections.
  Results: MT generally represents sounds compatible with the Chinese syllable structure of the period, whereas ST mainly encodes phonetic features less compatible with Chinese phonology. The analysis further shows that Chinese phonological categories were used more flexibly in foreign-language transcription than previously assumed. HHY therefore functioned as a relatively systematic method of phonetic approximation rather than a direct projection of Chinese phonology onto non-Chinese languages.
  Conclusion: HHY can be analyzed as an internally structured transcription system rather than merely as a collection of glossaries. More broadly, the study demonstrates that historical transcription systems can provide valuable evidence for historical phonology, particularly for under-documented Asian languages with limited historical records.

</details>


### [138] [GroupMemBench: Benchmarking LLM Agent Memory in Multi-Party Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14498)
*Jingbo Yang,Kwei-Herng Lai,Xiaowen Wang,Shiyu Chang,Yaar Harari,Evgeniy Gabrilovich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统及基准均基于单用户对话设计，忽略了多用户群组场景的三种关键特性：群组动态、说话者信念追踪、受众适配语言。作者提出GroupMemBench基准，通过图驱动的多轮对话合成与对抗性查询管道进行评估，发现最强系统仅达46.0%平均准确率，简单BM25基线即可匹敌或超越多数智能体记忆系统，表明当前记忆摄入机制严重破坏群组记忆所需的词汇与结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统与基准评测均局限于单用户（dyadic）对话场景，但实际部署中智能体常需在群组与频道中与多用户交互。这一鸿沟导致三项群组记忆关键能力未被衡量：超越简单一对一对话拼接的群组动态、基于说话者的信念追踪、以及需要心理理论（Theory-of-Mind）转换的受众适配语言。

Method: 提出GroupMemBench基准，包含两个核心管道：(1) 图驱动的合成管道，基于图结构生成可控回复拓扑的多轮群组对话，每条消息同时条件于用户个性化角色与目标受众；(2) 对抗性查询管道，从六类推理任务（多跳推理、知识更新、术语歧义、用户隐式推理、时序推理、弃权）中生成查询，每类问题绑定特定提问者，并通过迭代搜索寻找兼具挑战性与真实性的查询。

Result: 对主流智能体记忆系统的评测结果显示：最强系统仅达46.0%的平均准确率，其中知识更新类仅27.1%，术语歧义类仅37.7%。简单BM25基线表现与多数智能体记忆系统持平甚至更优，表明当前记忆摄入机制在信息抽取与存储过程中丢失了群组记忆所依赖的结构与词汇特征。

Conclusion: 多用户群组记忆问题远未解决，现有记忆系统在群组场景下存在根本性缺陷。简单检索基线（BM25）的表现反衬出当前智能体记忆系统在信息摄入环节破坏了群组交互的结构性与词汇性特征，亟需针对群组记忆特性设计新的记忆建模方法。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly serve as personal assistants and workplace collaborators, where their utility depends on memory systems that extract, retrieve, and apply information across long-running conversations. However, both existing memory systems and benchmarks are built around the dyadic, single-user setup, even though real deployments routinely span groups and channels with multiple users interacting with the agent and with each other. This mismatch leaves three properties of group memory unmeasured: (i) group dynamics that go beyond concatenated one-on-one chats, (ii) speaker-grounded belief tracking, where the per-user memory modeling is needed, and (iii) audience-adapted language, where Theory-of-Mind shifts produce role-specific vocabulary. We introduce GroupMemBench, a benchmark that exposes all three. A graph-grounded synthesis pipeline produces multi-party conversations with controllable reply structure and conditions each message on per-user personas and target audiences. An adversarial query pipeline then binds every question to a specific asker across six categories, spanning multi-hop reasoning, knowledge update, term ambiguity, user-implicit reasoning, temporal reasoning, and abstention, and iteratively searches challenging, realistic queries that reflect comprehensive memory capability. Benchmarking leading memory systems exposes a sharp collapse: the strongest one reaches only 46.0% average accuracy, with knowledge update at 27.1% and term ambiguity at 37.7%, while a simple BM25 baseline matches or exceeds most agent memory systems. This indicates current memory ingestion erases the structural and lexical features group memory depends on, leaving multi-user memory far from solved.

</details>


### [139] [Language Generation as Optimal Control: Closed-Loop Diffusion in Latent Control Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14531)
*ZiYi Dong,Yuliang Huang,Weijian Deng,Xiangyang Ji,Liang Lin,Pengxu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将语言生成重新形式化为随机最优控制问题，通过HJB方程近似求解得到闭环最优策略，并利用流匹配（Flow Matching）在整流潜在控制空间求解，提出Manta-LM全局积分算子模型，同时实现高保真文本生成与高效低成本并行采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归模型和扩散模型存在效率-保真度悖论、不可逆误差传播、优化可追踪性与保真度之间的权衡等问题，缺乏统一的理论视角来解释这些局限性。

Method: 1) 将语言生成建模为随机最优控制问题；2) 近似求解Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程得到闭环最优策略；3) 采用流匹配（Flow Matching）作为整流潜在控制空间中的最优轨迹求解器；4) 提出Manta-LM，利用全局积分算子逼近全局向量场。

Result: 在语言建模和条件生成任务上取得了强性能，同时展现出改进的稳定性、效率和可控性。

Conclusion: 通过最优控制理论与流匹配的结合，成功突破了自回归与扩散模型的固有局限，实现了高保真与高效并行采样的统一。

Abstract: This work reformulates language generation as a stochastic optimal control problem, providing a unified theoretical perspective to analyze autoregressive and diffusion models and explain their limitations (Efficiency-Fidelity Paradox, Irreversibility Error Propagation, Optimization Tractability and Fidelity) in terms of combination of trajectory singularity, adjoint state vanishing, and gradient absence. To address these issues, we approximate the solution to the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation, yielding an optimal policy that acts as a closed-loop controller. To bypass the intractability of directly solving the HJB PDE, we employ Flow Matching as the optimal trajectory solver within the rectified latent control space. This allows our Manta-LM with Global Integral Operator to approximate the global vector field, effectively realizing a model that simultaneously achieves high-fidelity text generation and efficient, low-cost parallel sampling. Empirically, our method achieves strong performance on language modeling and conditional generation tasks, while exhibiting improved stability, efficiency, and controllability.

</details>


### [140] [Learning from Failures: Correction-Oriented Policy Optimization with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14539)
*Mengjie Ren,Jie Lou,Boxi Cao,Xueru Wen,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Xing Yu,Yaojie Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Correction-Oriented Policy Optimization (CIPO)，一种简单有效的RLVR扩展方法，通过将同策略失败轨迹转化为修正导向的监督信号，在不依赖外部信号的情况下联合优化修正样本和标准RLVR目标，在11个数学推理和代码生成基准上显著提升推理和修正性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR训练受限于稀疏二元奖励和弱信用分配，导致优化信号模糊，未能充分利用失败轨迹中的有用信息。

Method: CIPO将模型自身同策略失败轨迹转化为修正导向的监督信号，联合优化修正样本与标准RLVR目标，无需任何外部信号。

Result: 在11个数学推理和代码生成基准上，CIPO持续且显著优于强基线方法，在推理和修正性能上均有提升，且pass@K增益更强。

Conclusion: CIPO通过利用失败轨迹改善了模型的推理能力，证明其提升的是模型内在推理能力而非简单重分配概率质量。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as an effective paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. However, RLVR training is often hindered by sparse binary rewards and weak credit assignment, resulting in ambiguous optimization signals and underutilization of the useful information embedded in failed trajectories. To address this challenge, we propose Correction-Oriented Policy Optimization (CIPO), a simple and effective extension to RLVR that converts on-policy failed trajectories into correction-oriented supervision, without relying on any external signals. By jointly optimizing correction samples derived from the model's own failed attempts together with the standard RLVR objective, CIPO improves learning effectiveness while explicitly enhancing the model's ability to correct its own errors. Extensive experiments across 11 benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning and code generation demonstrate that CIPO consistently and significantly outperforms strong baselines in both reasoning and correction performance. Moreover, CIPO yields stronger pass@K gains, indicating that it improves the model's intrinsic reasoning capacity rather than merely redistributing probability mass over existing correct answers.

</details>


### [141] [Uncertainty Quantification for Large Language Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14570)
*Artem Vazhentsev,Vladislav Smirnov,David Li,Maxim Panov,Timothy Baldwin,Artem Shelmanov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了大型语言扩散模型(LLDM)的不确定性量化(UQ)问题，提出了轻量级、零样本的不确定性信号，利用迭代去噪过程中的中间生成、token重掩码动态和去噪复杂度。通过将掩码扩散似然与轨迹语义不相似性结合，在三个任务、八个数据集和两个模型上实现了接近最强基线但计算开销低100倍的性能，证明LLDM可同时实现快速推理和可靠幻觉检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLDM作为自回归模型的高效替代方案，同样存在幻觉问题，但现有UQ方法要么依赖自回归分解，要么需要昂贵的重复采样，与LLDM的高效性相悖，亟需为LLDM设计专门的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 提出轻量级零样本不确定性信号，包括：1) 利用迭代去噪过程中的中间生成、token重掩码动态、去噪复杂度；2) 将掩码扩散似然与轨迹语义不相似性结合的适应化UQ方法；3) 理论证明期望轨迹不相似性下界于掩码扩散训练目标，为其作为不确定性分数提供理论依据。

Result: 在三个任务、八个数据集、两个模型上的实验表明，所提方法实现了优异的成本-性能权衡：接近最强采样基线的性能，同时计算开销降低高达100倍。

Conclusion: LLDM能够同时实现快速推理和可靠幻觉检测，解决了LLDM在高效部署中不确定性量化的关键挑战。

Abstract: Large Language Diffusion Models (LLDMs) are emerging as an alternative to autoregressive models, offering faster inference through higher parallelism. Similar to autoregressive LLMs, they remain prone to hallucinations, making reliable uncertainty quantification (UQ) crucial for safe deployment. However, existing UQ methods are fundamentally misaligned with this new paradigm: they assume autoregressive factorization or use expensive repeated sampling, negating the efficiency of LLDMs. In this work, we present the first systematic study of UQ for LLDMs and propose lightweight, zero-shot uncertainty signals derived from the iterative denoising process, leveraging intermediate generations, token remasking dynamics, and denoising complexity. We further adapt a state-of-the-art UQ method to LLDMs by combining masked diffusion likelihoods with trajectory-based semantic dissimilarity. We prove that expected trajectory dissimilarity lower bounds the masked diffusion training objective, which motivates its usage as an uncertainty score. Comprehensive experiments across three tasks, eight datasets, and two models show that our method achieves a great cost-performance trade-off: it approaches the strongest sampling-based baselines while incurring up to 100x lower computational overhead. Our work demonstrates that LLDMs can deliver both fast inference and reliable hallucination detection simultaneously.

</details>


### [142] [EndPrompt: Efficient Long-Context Extension via Terminal Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14589)
*Han Tian,Luxuan Chen,Xinran Chen,Rui Kong,Fang Wang,Jiamin Chen,Jinman Zhao,Yuchen Li,Jiashu Zhao,Shuaiqiang Wang,Haoyi Xiong,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EndPrompt提出了一种仅使用短训练序列即可实现大语言模型长上下文扩展的方法，通过在短序列中构造长距离相对位置编码，在LLaMA模型上将上下文窗口从8K扩展到64K，性能优于全长度微调等方法，且计算成本大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统长上下文扩展需要在目标长度的序列上进行训练，面临二次方内存和计算开销，使得长上下文适应昂贵且难以复现。作者希望探索是否可以通过稀疏的位置监督（而非密集的长序列训练）来实现有效的上下文窗口扩展。

Method: 将原始短上下文作为完整的第一个片段，附加一个简短的终端提示作为第二个片段，并为其分配接近目标上下文长度的位置索引。这种两段式结构在短物理序列内同时引入局部和长距离相对位置距离，同时保持训练文本的语义连续性。基于旋转位置编码（RoPE）和Bernstein不等式提供了理论分析，证明位置插值对注意力函数施加了严格的平滑性约束。

Result: 在LLaMA系列模型上将上下文窗口从8K扩展到64K，EndPrompt在RULER上平均得分76.03，在LongBench上取得最高平均分，优于LCEG（72.24）、LongLoRA（72.95）和全长度微调（69.23），同时计算量显著更低。

Conclusion: 长上下文泛化可以从稀疏的位置监督中诱导出来，挑战了"可靠上下文窗口扩展需要密集长序列训练"的主流假设。

Abstract: Extending the context window of large language models typically requires training on sequences at the target length, incurring quadratic memory and computational costs that make long-context adaptation expensive and difficult to reproduce. We propose EndPrompt, a method that achieves effective context extension using only short training sequences. The core insight is that exposing a model to long-range relative positional distances does not require constructing full-length inputs: we preserve the original short context as an intact first segment and append a brief terminal prompt as a second segment, assigning it positional indices near the target context length. This two-segment construction introduces both local and long-range relative distances within a short physical sequence while maintaining the semantic continuity of the training text--a property absent in chunk-based simulation approaches that split contiguous context.
